## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去看看某个仓库里挂掉的测试，弄清原因后修好。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a stuck test suite in one of your repos passing again.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 내일 아침에 또 얘기해요. 좋은 밤 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 좋은 밤 보내세요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視する通信」、UDPは「速さを重視する通信」です。TCPは届いたか・順番どおりかを確認して再送しますが、UDPはその確認をせず、速く送るだけです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, sobre todo en una rama que solo tú usas. Usa **merge** para integrar ramas que ya están publicadas o que otras personas usan, porque reescribir su historia rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 可靠：它会确认、重传并保证按序送达；UDP 不做这些保证，只管尽快发出去，所以更快、开销更小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to replay it on top of the latest base and keep history linear. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits other people may have pulled.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。
  [partner ack line] 好，我按顺序做：先换登录表单的输入框，接着接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，每件做完告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换新输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"folder","path":"~/dev/ducter","device":"local"} | runs on computer: the home (Ducter's home, called "local" in a place) | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 本任务只做第一件：把登录表单换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎ 决定：沿用仓库里已有的新输入框组件，不另造新组件。 ⏎ 完成标准：登录表单全部使用新输入框组件；现有测试和类型检查通过；在报告里给出测试输出作为证据。 ⏎ 范围外：不接短信验证码（下一件做）；不补端到端测试（最后一件做）；不推送、不合并、不开 PR；不改其他页面。 ⏎ 工作位置：~/dev/ducter。改动提交在自己的分支上。
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：接短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 登录页：换新输入框组件 (working); 2. 登录页：接短信验证码 (working); 3. 登录页：补端到端测试 (working)

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名或路径片段，Bun 会按名字匹配：

```bash
bun test parse
```

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "解析空字符串"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records each change to a database in an append-only file before the change is applied to the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log on restart to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones, so data survives without rewriting the full data files on every commit.
```

});

    var amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!isFinite(amt) || amt <= 0) {
      $('amt').focus();
      return;
    }
    var date = $('date').value.trim() || todayStr();
    spends.push({
      id: Date.now(),
      amt: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100,
      what: $('what').value.trim(),
      cat: cat,
      date: date
    });
    $('amt').value = '';
    $('what').value = '';
    $('date').value = todayStr();
    $('save').textContent = 'Saved';
    setTimeout(function () { $('save').textContent = 'Save'; }, 1200);
    save();
  };

  $('date').value = todayStr();
  buildChips();

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('spends')).then(function (v) {
    spends = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(function (v) {
    spends = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">今天已喝</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">今天的奶</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>

  <details class="k-add" id="addbox">
    <summary id="addtitle">记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="t">时间（如 06:30）</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="06:30">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="ml">毫升</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="120">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
    <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删掉这一条</button>
  </details>
</main>

<style>
  .k-app { background: var(--d-bg); color: var(--d-ink); padding: 16px; font-family: inherit; }
  .k-sum { background: var(--d-card); border-radius: 14px; padding: 16px; margin-bottom: 16px; }
  .k-sum-label { color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 14px; }
  .k-sum-value { font-size: 32px; font-weight: 600; margin: 4px 0; }
  .k-sum-sub { color: var(--d-soft); font-size: 14px; }
  .k-none { color: var(--d-muted); fo … nt = '改这一条';
    document.getElementById('save').textContent = '改好了';
    document.getElementById('del').hidden = false;
    document.getElementById('addbox').open = true;
  }

  function validTime(s) { return /^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(s); }

  document.getElementById('save').onclick = function () {
    var t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
    var ml = Number(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if (!validTime(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var hm = t.split(':');
    t = pad(Number(hm[0])) + ':' + hm[1];
    var list = load();
    if (editingId !== null) {
      list = list.map(function (f) {
        return f.id === editingId ? { id: f.id, day: f.day, time: t, ml: ml } : f;
      });
    } else {
      list.push({ id: Date.now(), day: todayStr(new Date()), time: t, ml: ml });
    }
    save(list);
    resetForm();
    document.getElementById('addbox').open = false;
  };

  document.getElementById('del').onclick = function () {
    if (editingId === null) return;
    save(load().filter(function (f) { return f.id !== editingId; }));
    resetForm();
    document.getElementById('addbox').open = false;
  };

  resetForm();
  render();
  ducter.onChange(render);
</script>

(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.

  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2348 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <span class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</span>
  <span class="k-sum-value" id="left">—</span>
  <span class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tick each one as it lands</span>
</div>

<h2 class="k-h">My PRs waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="waiting"></div></div>

<h2 class="k-h">Not waiting yet</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="other"></div></div>

<style>
.k-row{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:2px 0;cursor:pointer}
.k-row input[type=checkbox]{width:18px;height:18px;margin:0 10px 0 0;accent-color:var(--d-accent,#2b6cb0);flex:none}
.k-row-main{flex:1 1 0;min-width:0}
.k-row-sub{width:100%;padding-left:28px;box-sizing:border-box;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-done .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>

<script>
var DEFAULT=ducter.get("default") || [];
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
function render(items,done){
  var w='',o='',left=0;
  items.forEach(function(it){
    var checked=!!done[it.id];
    var row='<label class="k-row'+(checked?' k-done':'')+'"><input type="checkbox" data-id="'+esc(it.id)+'"'+(checked?' checked':'')+(it.waiting?'':' disabled')+'><span class="k-row-main">'+esc(it.title)+'</span><span class="k-row-sub">'+esc(it.sub)+'</span></label>';
    if(it.waiting){ w+=row; if(!checked) left++; } else { o+=row; }
  });
  document.getElementById('waiting').innerHTML=w;
  document.getElementById('other').innerHTML=o;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent=left;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent=left?'of '+items.filter(function(i){return i.waiting;}).length+' still waiting':'all landed';
  if(typeof ducter.summary==='function') ducter.summary(left+' waiting');
  document.querySelectorAll('input[data-id]').forEach(function(cb){
    cb.addEventListener('change',function(){
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function(d){
        d=d||{}; d[cb.dataset.id]=cb.checked; ducter.set('done',d);
        render(items,d);
      });
    });
  });
}
function load(){
  Promise.all([ducter.get('items'),ducter.get('done')]).then(function(r){
    render(r[0]||DEFAULT, r[1]||{});
  });
}
load();
if(typeof ducter.onChange==='function') ducter.onChange(load);
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"default":[{"id":"412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · Compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","waiting":true},{"id":"10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · Retry on 502","sub":"On CI, failing","waiting":true},{"id":"1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · Flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","waiting":true},{"id":"418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · Apps bar","sub":"Still a draft","waiting":false}],"items":[{"id":"412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · Compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","waiting":true},{"id":"10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · Retry on 502","sub":"On CI, failing","waiting":true},{"id":"1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · Flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","waiting":true},{"id":"418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · Apps bar","sub":"Still a draft","waiting":false}]}

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">这趟出差花了</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" data-c="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" data-c="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div></details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通";
const money=n=>"¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US");
const esc=s=>String(s??"").replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.c;document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(x=>x.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(x===b)))});
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
const amt=parseFloat(document.getElementById("amt").value);if(!amt)return;
const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
xs.push({trip:document.getElementById("trip").value||"出差",day:document.getElementById("day").value,cat,amt});
ducter.set("items",xs);document.getElementById("amt").value="";render();};
function render(){
const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt),0);
const tv=document.getElementById("total");
if(xs.length){tv.textContent=money(total);document.getElementById("sub").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";}
else{tv.innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';document.getElementById("sub").textContent="记下第一笔就有合计";}
document.getElementById("list").innerHTML=xs.length?xs.map(x=>'<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.cat)+' · '+esc(x.trip)+'<div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+(x.from?' <span class="k-from">'+esc(x.from)+'</span>':'')+'</div></div><div class="k-row-value">'+money(x.amt)+'</div></div>').join(""):'<div class="k-empty">还没有花销,展开下面记一笔</div>';
ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(total):"还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(render);render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20–21 · 亚朵酒店(望京)","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"from":"你的预订"}]}

背景：你上一轮修好了 foo 登录页的重定向循环（登录后 next 参数没校验，回跳到 /login 自己；已加白名单和 3 个测试，Chrome 下全过）。

要做的：
